// Splitter regexes (sr:) match the ORIGINAL text before substitutions; each named group is translated on its own
// (dictionary -> regex -> these rules again -> Google). Groups ending in _i are passed through untouched (numbers, tags).
// XUnity tries splitters from the BOTTOM of the loaded list upward — later lines win. Generic rules at the top, specific at the bottom.
